Result: 在最先进的视觉语言模型上的广泛实验表明，该方法能有效隐藏目标对象，同时保留高达86%的非目标对象，与基于注意力抑制的攻击相比，将基础幻觉减少了最多3倍。

Conclusion: 背景一致的对象隐藏攻击通过避免语义不连续性，显著减少了视觉语言模型中的幻觉问题，为对抗性视觉操作提供了更有效的方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently shown remarkable capabilities in visual understanding and generation, but remain vulnerable to adversarial manipulations of visual content. Prior object-hiding attacks primarily rely on suppressing or blocking region-specific representations, often creating semantic gaps that inadvertently induce hallucination, where models invent plausible but incorrect objects. In this work, we demonstrate that hallucination arises not from object absence per se, but from semantic discontinuity introduced by such suppression-based attacks. We propose a new class of \emph{background-consistent object concealment} attacks, which hide target objects by re-encoding their visual representations to be statistically and semantically consistent with surrounding background regions. Crucially, our approach preserves token structure and attention flow, avoiding representational voids that trigger hallucination. We present a pixel-level optimization framework that enforces background-consistent re-encoding across multiple transformer layers while preserving global scene semantics. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art vision-language models show that our method effectively conceals target objects while preserving up to $86\%$ of non-target objects and reducing grounded hallucination by up to $3\times$ compared to attention-suppression-based attacks.

</details>


### [77] [Cross-Scale Persistence Analysis of EM Side-Channels for Reference-Free Detection of Always-On Hardware Trojans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16058)
*Mahsa Tahghigh,Hassan Salmani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于电磁侧信道多尺度持续性分析的参考无关硬件木马检测框架，针对常开寄生硬件行为，通过跨尺度稳定性特征区分正常设计与硬件木马。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常开硬件木马在正常操作期间保持活动状态，在缺乏可信黄金参考的后部署环境中难以检测，对集成电路信任构成严重挑战。

Method: 提出基于电磁侧信道跨尺度持续性分析的参考无关检测框架：分析多时间-频率分辨率的电磁辐射，构建捕获重复执行中频谱特征一致性的稳定性映射；使用基于贝叶斯信息准则的高斯混合模型进行模型选择，表征每个尺度的统计结构；引入跨尺度饱和、变异性和中位数混合复杂度指标，量化统计结构是自然演化还是跨分辨率持续锚定。

Result: AES实现实验表明：无木马设计表现出与瞬态切换行为一致的尺度依赖性变异性，而常开木马产生抑制跨尺度演化的持续性统计特征；不同木马类别（如工作负载相关泄漏信息木马和独立环形振荡器木马）表现出不同的持续性模式。

Conclusion: 跨尺度持续性为无监督、参考无关的常开硬件木马检测提供了物理可解释且鲁棒的保证信号，能够有效区分正常设计与恶意硬件行为。

Abstract: Always-on hardware Trojans pose a serious challenge to integrated circuit trust, as they remain active during normal operation and are difficult to detect in post-deployment settings without trusted golden references. This paper presents a reference-free detection framework based on cross-scale persistence analysis of electromagnetic (EM) side-channels, targeting always-on parasitic hardware behavior. The proposed method analyzes EM emissions across multiple time-frequency resolutions and constructs stability maps that capture the consistency of spectral features over repeated executions. Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) with Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) based model selection are used to characterize statistical structure at each scale. We introduce cross-scale saturation, variability, and median mixture complexity metrics that quantify whether statistical structure evolves naturally or remains persistently anchored across resolutions. Experimental results on AES implementations show that Trojan-free designs exhibit scale-dependent variability consistent with transient switching behavior, while always-on Trojans produce persistent statistical signatures that suppress cross-scale evolution. Furthermore, different Trojan classes, such as workload-correlated leakage-information Trojans and independent ring-oscillator Trojans, exhibit distinct persistence patterns. These findings demonstrate that cross-scale persistence provides a physically interpretable and robust assurance signal for unsupervised, reference-free detection of always-on hardware Trojans.

</details>


### [78] [Resource Consumption Threats in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16068)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Xinyue Wang,Zhican Chen,Weiliu Wang,Zilu Zhang,Zhengshuo Gong,Zhenhong Zhou,Kun Wang,Li Sun,Yang Liu,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了大型语言模型中的资源消耗威胁，建立了从威胁诱导到机制理解再到缓解的统一框架，旨在为这一新兴领域提供清晰的问题界定和缓解基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算基础设施有限且成本高昂的背景下，资源效率是大型语言模型的关键要求。高效的LLM能够提高服务提供商的容量，降低用户的延迟和API成本。然而，最近的资源消耗威胁导致过度生成，降低了模型效率，损害了服务可用性和经济可持续性。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，对LLM中的资源消耗威胁进行全面审查。建立统一视角，通过澄清问题范围，并沿着从威胁诱导到机制理解和缓解的完整流程来审视问题。

Result: 该调查为LLM资源消耗威胁这一新兴领域提供了系统性的问题界定和分类框架，建立了从威胁诱导到缓解的完整分析流程，为后续的特征描述和缓解措施提供了清晰的基础。

Conclusion: LLM资源消耗威胁是一个重要且新兴的研究领域，需要系统性的理解和缓解策略。该调查通过建立统一框架，为研究人员和从业者提供了明确的问题界定和分析路径，有助于提高LLM的资源效率和系统可持续性。

Abstract: Given limited and costly computational infrastructure, resource efficiency is a key requirement for large language models (LLMs). Efficient LLMs increase service capacity for providers and reduce latency and API costs for users. Recent resource consumption threats induce excessive generation, degrading model efficiency and harming both service availability and economic sustainability. This survey presents a systematic review of threats to resource consumption in LLMs. We further establish a unified view of this emerging area by clarifying its scope and examining the problem along the full pipeline from threat induction to mechanism understanding and mitigation. Our goal is to clarify the problem landscape for this emerging area, thereby providing a clearer foundation for characterization and mitigation.

</details>


### [79] [Ciphertext-Policy ABE for $\mathsf{NC}^1$ Circuits with Constant-Size Ciphertexts from Succinct LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16117)
*Jiaqi Liu,Yuanyi Zhang,Fang-Wei Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于格的密文策略属性基加密方案，支持NC¹访问策略，具有恒定大小的密文，并应用于广播加密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有属性基加密方案通常具有较大的密文尺寸，特别是对于复杂访问策略。本文旨在构建一个基于格的CP-ABE方案，在保持安全性的同时实现恒定大小的密文，这对于实际应用中的存储和传输效率至关重要。

Method: 采用基于格的密码学构造，利用Wee在CRYPTO 2024中引入的多项式简洁LWE假设。方案针对NC¹电路访问策略设计，通过巧妙的数学结构实现公钥和密文尺寸与电路深度无关的恒定大小，同时密钥尺寸与输入长度线性相关。

Result: 成功构建了一个支持NC¹访问策略的CP-ABE方案：公钥和密文尺寸为O(1)（独立于电路深度d），密钥尺寸为O(ℓ)。作为应用，获得了支持N个用户的广播加密方案，密文尺寸为poly(λ)（独立于log N），密钥尺寸为poly(λ, log N)。

Conclusion: 本文提出了第一个基于格的具有恒定大小密文的CP-ABE方案，支持NC¹访问策略，在标准模型下基于多项式简洁LWE假设实现选择性安全。该方案为高效属性基加密和广播加密提供了新的构造方法。

Abstract: We construct a lattice-based ciphertext-policy attribute-based encryption (CP-ABE) scheme for $\mathsf{NC}^1$ access policies with constant-size ciphertexts. Let $λ$ be the security parameter. For an $\mathsf{NC}^1$ circuit of depth $d$ and size $s$ on $\ell$-bit inputs, our scheme has the public-key and ciphertext sizes $O(1)$ (independent of $d$), and secret-key size $O(\ell)$, where the $O(\cdot)$ hides $\operatorname{poly}(λ)$ factors. As an application, we obtain a broadcast encryption scheme for $N$ users with ciphertext size $\operatorname{poly}(λ)$ independent of $\log N$ and key sizes $\operatorname{poly}(λ,\log N)$. Our construction is selectively secure in the standard model under the $\operatorname{poly}(λ)$-succinct LWE assumption introduced by Wee (CRYPTO~2024).

</details>


### [80] [Novel CRT-based Asymptotically Ideal Disjunctive Hierarchical Secret Sharing Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16267)
*Hongju Li,Jian Ding,Fuyou Miao,Cheng Wang,Cheng Shu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于中国剩余定理(CRT)的析取层次秘密共享(DHSS)方案，指出现有方案存在安全漏洞或信息率不足的问题，并提出了一种新的渐近完美CRT-DHSS方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRT-based DHSS方案存在安全缺陷：Yang等人(2024)的理想方案和Tiplea等人(2021)的渐近理想方案都不安全，导致现有方案要么有安全漏洞，要么信息率低于1/2。需要设计既安全又高效的DHSS方案。

Method: 提出了一种基于中国剩余定理的渐近完美DHSS方案，支持灵活的份额大小。当所有份额大小相等时，该方案是渐近理想的。方案具有计算安全性。

Result: 新方案的信息率达到1，实现了渐近完美性。在份额大小相等的情况下达到渐近理想状态，同时保持了计算安全性。

Conclusion: 成功解决了现有CRT-based DHSS方案的安全性和效率问题，提出的新方案既安全又高效，为析取层次秘密共享提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Disjunctive Hierarchical Secret Sharing (DHSS)} scheme is a type of secret sharing scheme in which the set of all participants is partitioned into disjoint subsets, and each subset is said to be a level with different degrees of trust and different thresholds. In this work, we focus on the Chinese Remainder Theorem (CRT)-based DHSS schemes due to their ability to accommodate flexible share sizes. We point out that the ideal DHSS scheme of Yang et al. (ISIT, 2024) and the asymptotically ideal DHSS scheme of Tiplea et al. (IET Information Security, 2021) are insecure. Consequently, existing CRT-based DHSS schemes either exhibit security flaws or have an information rate less than $\frac{1}{2}$. To address these limitations, we propose a CRT-based asymptotically perfect DHSS scheme that supports flexible share sizes. Notably, our scheme is asymptotically ideal when all shares are equal in size. Its information rate achieves one and it has computational security.

</details>


### [81] [Systematization of Knowledge: The Design Space of Digital Payment Systems with Potential for CBDC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16320)
*Judith Senn,Aljosha Judmayer,Nicholas Stifter,Rainer Böhme*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文对36个支付系统设计方案进行系统性知识梳理，为央行数字货币设计提供技术分析，识别设计模式、技术权衡和实施挑战，并指出离线支付和后量子安全等研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 央行数字货币作为对私有数字支付兴起的公共回应，为重新思考货币未来提供了机会。虽然央行正在研究隐私增强技术，但往往认为这些技术不成熟或扩展性不足，且缺乏对完整系统规模化的深入分析。

Method: 采用结构化、自上而下的技术分析方法，对36个完整的支付系统设计方案进行系统性知识梳理，这些方案可为CBDC设计提供参考或专门为此应用提出。

Result: 识别了重复出现的设计模式、技术权衡和实施挑战，为CBDC设计提供了系统化的技术见解和分析框架。

Conclusion: 研究强调了当前CBDC设计中的研究空白，特别是离线支付和后量子安全领域，为未来研究方向提供了重要指引。

Abstract: Central Bank Digital Currencies (CBDCs) are proposed as a public response to the uptake of privately run digital payments, with the digital euro, under development by the European Central Bank (ECB), serving as a prominent example. This momentum provides a unique opportunity to fundamentally rethink the future of money, and, assuming wide adoption, to establish payment systems that offer strong cryptographic security and privacy guarantees from the start. While the central banks in charge are investigating privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs), they often conclude that PETs are immature or insufficiently scalable. Moreover, these efforts tend to examine primitives in isolation, offering little insight into how a system using these PETs would scale. This systematisation of knowledge, therefore, provides a structured, top-down technical analysis of 36 payment system designs of complete system proposals that can inform CBDC designs or were explicitly proposed for this application. We identify recurring design patterns, technical trade-offs, and implementation challenges. Concluding, we highlight research gaps, including offline payments and post-quantum security.

</details>


### [82] [SseRex: Practical Symbolic Execution of Solana Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16349)
*Tobias Cloosters,Pascal Winkler,Jens-Rene Giesen,Ghassan Karame,Lucas Davi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SseRex是首个针对Solana智能合约的符号执行漏洞检测方法，专门检测Solana特有的安全漏洞，在8714个字节码合约中发现了467个潜在漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana的快速普及伴随着一系列重大安全事件，现有分析方法未能解决Solana独特且复杂的账户模型，需要专门的漏洞检测工具。

Method: 提出SseRex方法，使用符号执行技术检测Solana特有的安全漏洞，包括缺失所有者检查、缺失签名者检查、缺失密钥检查以及任意跨程序调用等漏洞类型。

Result: 在8714个仅字节码合约的评估中，SseRex优于现有方法，识别出467个不同合约中的潜在漏洞。对120个开源Solana项目的分析显示，细微且容易被忽视的问题往往是严重漏洞的根本原因。

Conclusion: SseRex作为首个针对Solana的符号执行漏洞检测方法，有效解决了Solana特有安全漏洞的检测需求，突显了专门分析工具的必要性。

Abstract: Solana is rapidly gaining traction among smart contract developers and users. However, its growing adoption has been accompanied by a series of major security incidents, which have spurred research into automated analysis techniques for Solana smart contracts. Unfortunately, existing approaches do not address the unique and complex account model of Solana. In this paper, we propose SseRex, the first symbolic execution vulnerability detection approach for finding Solana-specific bugs such as missing owner checks, missing signer checks, and missing key checks, as well as arbitrary cross-program invocations. Our evaluation of 8,714 bytecode-only contracts shows that our approach outperforms existing approaches and identifies potential bugs in 467 different contracts. Additionally, we analyzed 120 open-source Solana projects and conducted in-depth case studies on four of them. Our findings reveal that subtle, easily overlooked issues often serve as the root cause of severe exploits, further highlighting the need for specialized analysis tools like SseRex.

</details>


### [83] [Impact of File-Open Hook Points on Backup Ratio in ROFBS on XFS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16364)
*Kosuke Higuchi,Ryotaro Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ROFBS防御机制中钩子点选择对勒索软件防护效果有显著影响，在XFS文件系统上使用xfs_file_open钩子点能最小化加密文件总数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件在攻击开始到检测之间的延迟期间持续加密文件，ROFBS通过在文件打开事件时实时备份修改前文件来缓解此问题。但由于Linux文件打开路径涉及多个内核函数，钩子点选择如何影响防御效果尚不明确

Method: 保持ROFBS机制不变，仅改变Linux文件打开路径上的钩子点：may_open、inode_permission、do_dentry_open、security_file_open和xfs_file_open。在AlmaLinux XFS系统上测试三种勒索软件家族：AvosLocker、Conti和IceFire。使用备份比率作为主要指标，同时比较有备份的加密文件数和总加密文件数

Result: 钩子点选择显著影响可恢复性和损害规模。对于AvosLocker，security_file_open获得最高备份比率(82.5%)。对于Conti和IceFire，xfs_file_open获得最高值(100.0%和63.2%)。此外，xfs_file_open对所有三种勒索软件家族都最小化了总加密文件数

Conclusion: 在ROFBS中，观察文件打开事件的层级是关键设计因素。特别是在XFS上，当目标是减少总体损害时，钩住文件系统特定回调xfs_file_open可能具有优势

Abstract: Ransomware continues encrypting files during the delay between attack onset and detection. ROFBS mitigates this problem by backing up pre-modification files in real time upon file-open events. However, because the Linux file-open path traverses multiple kernel functions, it remains unclear how the choice of hook point affects defense effectiveness.
  In this study, we kept the ROFBS mechanism fixed and changed only the hook points on the Linux file-open path. We compared may_open, inode_permission, do_dentry_open, security_file_open, and xfs_file_open on AlmaLinux with XFS using three ransomware families: AvosLocker, Conti, and IceFire. We used Backup Ratio as the main metric and also compared the number of encrypted files with backups and the total number of encrypted files.
  The results showed that hook-point selection substantially affected both recoverability and damage scale. For AvosLocker, security_file_open achieved the highest Backup Ratio (82.5%). For Conti and IceFire, xfs_file_open achieved the highest values (100.0% and 63.2%, respectively). Moreover, xfs_file_open minimized the total number of encrypted files for all three ransomware families.
  These results indicate that, in ROFBS, the layer at which file-open events are observed is a key design factor. In particular, on XFS, hooking the filesystem-specific callback xfs_file_open may be advantageous when the goal is to reduce overall damage.

</details>


### [84] [Rotated Robustness: A Training-Free Defense against Bit-Flip Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16382)
*Deng Liu,Song Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RoR（旋转鲁棒性）是一种无需训练的防御方法，通过正交Householder变换旋转激活空间，打破激活异常值与易受攻击权重比特之间的空间对齐，从而有效防御量化LLM中的比特翻转硬件故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化大语言模型中的硬件故障（权重比特翻转）会导致灾难性的模型崩溃。研究发现这种脆弱性源于敏感权重比特与极端激活异常值之间的空间对齐，使得单个硬件故障被大规模放大。

Method: 提出Rotated Robustness (RoR)方法，使用正交Householder变换对激活空间进行正交旋转。这种方法在几何上平滑所有特征维度上的极端异常值，打破异常值与脆弱权重之间的对齐，同时数学上保证原始模型精度。

Result: 在Llama-2/3、OPT和Qwen系列模型上的广泛实验表明：在随机比特翻转攻击下，RoR将Qwen2.5-7B的随机崩溃率从3.15%降至0.00%；在50个渐进比特搜索翻转的严重定向攻击下，RoR在Llama-2-7B上保持43.9%的MMLU准确率（接近未受攻击的45.2%）；针对最激进的单点故障攻击，RoR将攻击复杂度从几个比特指数级增加到超过17,000个精确比特翻转。

Conclusion: RoR以仅0.31%的存储开销和9.1%的推理延迟增加，实现了真正的无损鲁棒性，为LLM部署提供了实用且高度可靠的防御方案。

Abstract: Hardware faults, specifically bit-flips in quantized weights, pose a severe reliability threat to Large Language Models (LLMs), often triggering catastrophic model collapses. We demonstrate that this vulnerability fundamentally stems from the spatial alignment between sensitive weight bits and extreme activation outliers, which causes a single hardware fault to be massively amplified. To address this, we propose Rotated Robustness (RoR), a training-free defense utilizing orthogonal Householder transformations. By applying an orthogonal rotation to the activation space, RoR geometrically smooths extreme outliers across all feature dimensions. This mechanism effectively breaks the alignment between outliers and vulnerable weights, mathematically guaranteeing original model accuracy. Extensive empirical evaluations across Llama-2/3, OPT, and Qwen families demonstrate the superior reliability of our approach. Under random bit-flip attacks, RoR reduces the stochastic collapse rate from 3.15\% to 0.00\% on Qwen2.5-7B. Furthermore, under severe targeted attacks with 50 Progressive Bit Search flips, RoR sustains robust reasoning on Llama-2-7B, maintaining a 43.9\% MMLU accuracy that nearly matches its 45.2\% unattacked accuracy, while competing defenses collapse to random guessing. Most notably, against the Single-Point Fault Attack (SPFA) -- the most aggressive targeted threat -- RoR exponentially inflates the attack complexity from a few bits to over 17,000 precise bit-flips. With a negligible storage overhead of 0.31\% and a minimal inference latency increase of 9.1\% on Llama-2-7B, RoR achieves true lossless robustness, providing a practical and highly reliable defense for LLM deployment.

</details>


### [85] [SAMSEM -- A Generic and Scalable Approach for IC Metal Line Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16548)
*Christian Gehrmann,Jonas Ricker,Simon Damm,Deruo Cheng,Julian Speith,Yiqiong Shi,Asja Fischer,Christof Paar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAMSEM：基于SAM2的集成电路金属线分割模型，通过多尺度分割和拓扑损失实现跨技术节点、材料和成像技术的泛化能力，显著提升芯片制造后验证的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球化硬件供应链中，确保集成电路硬件组件的安全性至关重要，特别是在加密应用和高风险场景中。识别SEM图像中的金属线是验证芯片是否包含恶意电路的关键步骤。由于制造工艺和技术的差异，现有方法通常需要针对每个目标IC调整参数和算法，且在一个IC上训练的模型难以准确检测其他IC的金属线。

Method: 1. 将Meta的Segment Anything Model 2（SAM2）适配到IC金属线分割领域；2. 开发多尺度分割方法，处理不同尺寸、分辨率和放大倍数的SEM图像；3. 部署基于拓扑的损失函数与基于像素的损失函数相结合，专注于电气连通性而非像素级精度；4. 通过超参数优化对SAM2模型进行微调；5. 使用包含48个金属层、14个不同IC的SEM图像数据集进行训练和评估。

Result: 1. 在7个IC上微调后，对相同IC的其他图像评估错误率低至0.72%；2. 对剩余7个未见过的IC，错误率低至5.53%；3. 在所有14个IC上微调后，错误率为0.62%；4. 模型能够泛化到不同技术节点、制造材料、样品制备方法和SEM成像技术。

Conclusion: SAMSEM是一个可靠的工具，显著推进了金属线分割技术的前沿，解决了IC制造后验证中的关键挑战。该模型通过多尺度分割和拓扑损失实现了优异的跨IC泛化能力，为硬件安全验证提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In light of globalized hardware supply chains, the assurance of hardware components has gained significant interest, particularly in cryptographic applications and high-stakes scenarios. Identifying metal lines on scanning electron microscope (SEM) images of integrated circuits (ICs) is one essential step in verifying the absence of malicious circuitry in chips manufactured in untrusted environments. Due to varying manufacturing processes and technologies, such verification usually requires tuning parameters and algorithms for each target IC. Often, a machine learning model trained on images of one IC fails to accurately detect metal lines on other ICs. To address this challenge, we create SAMSEM by adapting Meta's Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) to the domain of IC metal line segmentation. Specifically, we develop a multi-scale segmentation approach that can handle SEM images of varying sizes, resolutions, and magnifications. Furthermore, we deploy a topology-based loss alongside pixel-based losses to focus our segmentation on electrical connectivity rather than pixel-level accuracy. Based on a hyperparameter optimization, we then fine-tune the SAM2 model to obtain a model that generalizes across different technology nodes, manufacturing materials, sample preparation methods, and SEM imaging technologies. To this end, we leverage an unprecedented dataset of SEM images obtained from 48 metal layers across 14 different ICs. When fine-tuned on seven ICs, SAMSEM achieves an error rate as low as 0.72% when evaluated on other images from the same ICs. For the remaining seven unseen ICs, it still achieves error rates as low as 5.53%. Finally, when fine-tuned on all 14 ICs, we observe an error rate of 0.62%. Hence, SAMSEM proves to be a reliable tool that significantly advances the frontier in metal line segmentation, a key challenge in post-manufacturing IC verification.

</details>


### [86] [SynthChain: A Synthetic Benchmark and Forensic Analysis of Advanced and Stealthy Software Supply Chain Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16694)
*Zhuoran Tan,Wenbo Guo,Taylor Brierley,Jiewen Luo,Jeremy Singer,Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SynthChain是一个近生产测试平台和多源运行时数据集，提供供应链攻击的链级真实标签，用于研究在有限遥测条件下的检测和关联能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件供应链攻击日益呈现运行时特性，证据分散在主机、服务和构建/依赖层，单一遥测流在现实访问和预算限制下无法完整重建攻击链

Method: 构建SynthChain测试平台，基于真实恶意软件包和攻击活动创建多源运行时数据集，涵盖PyPI、npm和C/C++供应链的7种攻击场景，跨Windows/Linux系统，涉及4台主机和1个容器环境，标注14种MITRE ATT&CK战术和161种技术

Result: 数据集包含约58万原始多源事件和150万评估行；最佳单一源仅能达到0.391加权标签/步骤覆盖率和0.403平均链重建率；最小双源融合可将覆盖率提升至0.636，重建率提升至0.639（约1.6倍增益），链覆盖/召回率提升至0.545

Conclusion: 单一遥测源无法完整覆盖供应链攻击链，多源融合显著提升检测和重建能力；发布的数据集和工具支持可重现的、具备取证意识的运行时防御研究，指导软件供应链的高效检测

Abstract: Advanced software supply chain (SSC) attacks are increasingly runtime-only and leave fragmented evidence across hosts, services, and build/dependency layers, so any single telemetry stream is inherently insufficient to reconstruct full compromise chains under realistic access and budget limits. We present SynthChain, a near-production testbed and a multi-source runtime dataset with chain-level ground truth, derived from real-world malicious packages and exploit campaigns. SynthChain covers seven representative supply-chain exploit scenarios across PyPI, npm, and a native C/C++ supply-chain case, spanning Windows and Linux, and involving four hosts and one containerized environment. Scenarios span realistic time windows from minutes to hours and are annotated with 14 MITRE ATT&CK tactics and 161 techniques (29-104 techniques per scenario). Beyond releasing the data, we quantify observability constraints by mapping each chain step to the minimum evidence needed for detection and cross-source correlation. With realistic trace availability, no single source is chain-complete: the best single source reaches only 0.391 weighted tag/step coverage and 0.403 mean chain reconstruction. Even minimal two-source fusion boosts coverage to 0.636 and reconstruction to 0.639 (approximately 1.6x gain), with consistent chain coverage/recall improvements (0.545). The corpus contains approximately 0.58M raw multi-source events and 1.50M evaluation rows, enabling controlled studies of detection under constrained telemetry. We release the dataset, ground truth, and artifacts to support reproducible, forensic-aware runtime defenses and to guide efficient detection for software supply chains.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Loosely-Structured Software: Engineering Context, Structure, and Evolution Entropy in Runtime-Rewired Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15690)
*Weihao Zhang,Yitong Zhou,Huanyu Qu,Hongyi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出松散结构软件(LSS)框架，通过三层工程架构管理基于LLM的多智能体系统中的运行时熵，提升系统的可设计性、可扩展性和可演化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的多智能体系统变得更加自主，其自由形式交互主导系统行为，但扩展智能体数量会放大上下文压力、协调错误和系统漂移。构建健壮的MAS不仅需要提示调优或增强模型智能，更需要专注于架构的工程纪律来管理不确定性下的复杂性。

Method: 提出松散结构软件(LSS)作为新软件类别，引入三层工程框架：视图/上下文工程管理执行环境和任务相关视图；结构工程组织工件和智能体的动态绑定；演化工程管理自重写工件的生命周期。基于此框架开发LSS设计模式作为语义控制块。

Result: 提供了关键机制的基本实验验证，证明LSS的有效性。这些抽象概念改善了智能体基础设施的可设计性、可扩展性和可演化性。

Conclusion: LSS框架将工程重点从构建确定性逻辑转向管理运行时熵，通过视图构造编程、语义驱动的自组织和内生演化来稳定推理介导的交互，同时保持智能体适应性，为构建健壮的多智能体系统提供了系统化的工程方法。

Abstract: As LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) become more autonomous, their free-form interactions increasingly dominate system behavior. However, scaling the number of agents often amplifies context pressure, coordination errors, and system drift. It is well known that building robust MAS requires more than prompt tuning or increased model intelligence. It necessitates engineering discipline focused on architecture to manage complexity under uncertainty. We characterize agentic software by a core property: \emph{runtime generation and evolution under uncertainty}. Drawing upon and extending software engineering experience, especially object-oriented programming, this paper introduces \emph{Loosely-Structured Software (LSS)}, a new class of software systems that shifts the engineering focus from constructing deterministic logic to managing the runtime entropy generated by View-constructed programming, semantic-driven self-organization, and endogenous evolution.
  To make this entropy governable, we introduce design principles under a three-layer engineering framework: \emph{View/Context Engineering} to manage the execution environment and maintain task-relevant Views, \emph{Structure Engineering} to organize dynamic binding over artifacts and agents, and \emph{Evolution Engineering} to govern the lifecycle of self-rewriting artifacts. Building on this framework, we develop LSS design patterns as semantic control blocks that stabilize fluid, inference-mediated interactions while preserving agent adaptability. Together, these abstractions improve the \emph{designability}, \emph{scalability}, and \emph{evolvability} of agentic infrastructure. We provide basic experimental validation of key mechanisms, demonstrating the effectiveness of LSS.

</details>


### [88] [VibeContract: The Missing Quality Assurance Piece in Vibe Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15691)
*Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出VibeContract范式，通过将自然语言意图分解为明确任务序列并生成任务级契约，为AI辅助编程提供结构化、可验证的质量保证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手生成的代码常包含隐藏逻辑错误，现有质量保证方法不足，需要新的QA方法来应对vibe coding带来的挑战。

Method: 将高层自然语言意图分解为明确任务序列，生成任务级契约捕获预期输入、输出、约束和行为属性，通过契约指导LLM进行测试、运行时验证和调试。

Result: 通过示例项目证明VibeContract范式能显著提高LLM生成代码的正确性、健壮性和可维护性，建立任务、契约和生成代码间的可追溯性。

Conclusion: VibeContract为vibe coding引入结构化、可验证的开发流程，将其从快速但易错实践转变为可预测、可审计、可信赖的软件工程过程，并提出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have given rise to vibe coding, a style of software development where developers rely on AI coding assistants to generate, modify, and refactor code using natural language instructions. While this paradigm accelerates software development and lowers barriers to entry, it introduces new challenges for quality assurance (QA). AI-generated code can appear correct but often contains hidden logical errors and inconsistencies, creating an urgent need for novel QA approaches. In this vision paper, we propose the VibeContract paradigm as a missing piece in vibe coding. In this approach, high-level natural-language intent is decomposed into explicit task sequences, and task-level contracts are generated to capture expected inputs, outputs, constraints, and behavioral properties. Developers validate these contracts, and traceability is maintained between tasks, contracts, and generated code. Contracts then guide LLMs for testing, runtime verification, and debugging, enabling QA to occur continuously and proactively alongside code generation. We demonstrate how the VibeContract paradigm can substantially improve the correctness, robustness, and maintainability of LLM-generated code through an example project. We argue that VibeContract introduces a structured and verifiable development workflow that transforms vibe coding from a fast but error-prone practice into a predictable, auditable, and trustworthy software engineering process. Finally, we outline the key challenges, design principles, and a forward-looking research agenda required to realize this vision.

</details>


### [89] [SEMAG: Self-Evolutionary Multi-Agent Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15707)
*Yulin Peng,Haowen Hou,Xinxin Zhu,Ying Tiffany He,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SEMAG是一个自进化的多智能体代码生成框架，通过模拟人类编程实践，将编程任务分解为规划、编码、调试和讨论等阶段，并能够根据任务难度自适应调整工作流程，同时实时访问最新模型并自动升级骨干模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在处理复杂编程任务时，依赖手动模型选择和固定工作流程，这限制了其适应不断变化的任务复杂性的能力。需要一种能够自适应调整并利用最新模型能力的框架。

Method: 提出SEMAG框架，将编程任务分解为规划、编码、调试和讨论等阶段，工作流程根据任务难度自适应调整。框架包含自进化智能体，能够实时访问最新模型并自动升级骨干模型，同时采用自进化模型选择机制自动识别最优骨干模型。

Result: SEMAG在多个基准测试中创造了新的Pass@1准确率记录。使用相同骨干模型时，在CodeContests上比先前方法高出3.3%。当结合自进化模型选择机制时，准确率达到52.6%，展示了框架的有效性和对不断发展的LLM能力的适应性。

Conclusion: SEMAG通过模拟人类编程实践的自进化多智能体框架，成功解决了当前代码生成方法在模型选择和流程适应性方面的限制，展现了在复杂编程任务上的优越性能和对LLM能力演进的适应性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in handling complex programming tasks. However, current methods rely on manual model selection and fixed workflows, which limit their ability to adapt to changing task complexities. To address this, we propose SEMAG, a Self-Evolutionary Multi-Agent code Generation framework that mimics human coding practices. It decomposes programming tasks into stages, including planning, coding, debugging, and discussion, while adapting workflows to task difficulty. Its self-evolutionary agents can access the latest models in real time and automatically upgrade the backbone model. SEMAG sets new state-of-the-art Pass@1 accuracy across benchmarks. Using identical backbone models, SEMAG outperforms prior methods by 3.3% on CodeContests. When augmented with self-evolutionary model selection that automatically identifies optimal backbones, SEMAG reaches 52.6%, showcasing both framework effectiveness and adaptability to evolving LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [90] [A Framework and Prototype for a Navigable Map of Datasets in Engineering Design and Systems Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15722)
*H. Sinan Bank,Daniel R. Herber*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统框架来创建"EDSE数据集地图"，通过多维分类法和知识图谱模型解决工程设计中数据碎片化和难以访问的问题，并识别当前数据景观中的"数据沙漠"和"数据绿洲"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计与系统工程领域的数据碎片化和难以访问阻碍了方法验证、可重复性和研究进展。与计算机视觉和自然语言处理等拥有成熟基准生态系统的领域不同，工程设计研究通常依赖小型、专有或临时数据集。

Method: 提出一个基于多维分类法的系统框架，按领域、生命周期阶段、数据类型和格式对工程数据集进行分类。设计了一个交互式发现工具的架构，采用知识图谱数据模型来捕获数据集、工具和出版物之间的丰富语义关系，并通过工作原型进行演示。

Result: 分析当前数据景观发现：早期设计和系统架构存在"数据沙漠"（数据不足区域），而预测性维护和自主系统则相对充足（"数据绿洲"）。提出了一个工作原型来展示该框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为解决工程设计中数据可访问性问题提供了系统方法，识别了数据景观中的不平衡现象，并提出了策展和可持续性方面的挑战及缓解策略，为加速数据驱动的工程研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The proliferation of data across the system lifecycle presents both a significant opportunity and a challenge for Engineering Design and Systems Engineering (EDSE). While this "digital thread" has the potential to drive innovation, the fragmented and inaccessible nature of existing datasets hinders method validation, limits reproducibility, and slows research progress. Unlike fields such as computer vision and natural language processing, which benefit from established benchmark ecosystems, engineering design research often relies on small, proprietary, or ad-hoc datasets. This paper addresses this challenge by proposing a systematic framework for a "Map of Datasets in EDSE." The framework is built upon a multi-dimensional taxonomy designed to classify engineering datasets by domain, lifecycle stage, data type, and format, enabling faceted discovery. An architecture for an interactive discovery tool is detailed and demonstrated through a working prototype, employing a knowledge graph data model to capture rich semantic relationships between datasets, tools, and publications. An analysis of the current data landscape reveals underrepresented areas ("data deserts") in early-stage design and system architecture, as well as relatively well-represented areas ("data oases") in predictive maintenance and autonomous systems. The paper identifies key challenges in curation and sustainability and proposes mitigation strategies, laying the groundwork for a dynamic, community-driven resource to accelerate data-centric engineering research.

</details>


### [91] [A Comparative Analysis of Backbone Algorithms for Configurable Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15833)
*Luis Cambelo,Ruben Heradio,Jose-Miguel Horcas,Dictino Chaos,David Fernandez-Amoros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次对真实世界可变性模型公式进行骨干算法综合评估，发现可变性模型公式结构独特，提供算法选择指南，并识别研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布尔公式骨干（所有满足赋值中必须为真的文字集合）对可变性模型的关键操作至关重要，但先前研究仅基于SAT竞赛公式评估算法，导致性能结论不一致，需要针对真实世界可变性模型公式进行首次全面评估。

Method: 分析5种最先进算法的21种配置，在2,371个来自可配置系统的公式上进行评估，公式规模从100变量/179子句到186,059变量/527,240子句，比较算法性能并分析公式结构特征。

Result: 可变性模型公式结构独特：子句密度更高但子句更简单。算法2/3（带解过滤的迭代算法）适用于1,000变量以下的公式，算法5（分块核心基础算法）配合自适应分块大小选择在大型公式上表现最佳。过滤启发式对可变性模型性能影响可忽略或负面。算法5在最优分块大小下可减少50%以上运行时间，但最优分块大小无法预测。

Conclusion: 为可变性模型骨干计算提供明确的算法选择指南，识别了关键研究空白：算法5的最优分块大小无法预测，为未来研究指明方向，强调需要开发能够估计最优分块大小的技术。

Abstract: The backbone of a Boolean formula is the set of literals that must be true in every assignment that satisfies the formula. This concept is fundamental to key operations on variability models, including propagating user configuration decisions to identify implied feature selections, detecting dead features and dead code blocks, and preprocessing formulas to accelerate knowledge compilation into tractable representations such as binary decision diagrams. Despite its importance, previous empirical studies have evaluated backbone algorithms solely on SAT competition formulas (typically engineered to test the limits of SAT solvers), leading to inconsistent conclusions about their performance. This study provides the first comprehensive evaluation of formulas derived from real-world variability models, analyzing 21 configurations of 5 state-of-the-art algorithms on 2,371 formulas from configurable systems ranging from 100 variables and 179 clauses to 186,059 variables and 527,240 clauses. The results indicate that variability model formulas are structurally distinct, with higher clause density but greater clause simplicity. Our research provides clear algorithm selection guidelines: Algorithm 2/3 (iterative with solution filtering) is recommended for formulas with 1,000 or fewer variables, while Algorithm 5 (chunked core-based) with adaptive chunk size selection provides the best practical performance for larger formulas. Also, the results show that filtering heuristics have negligible or negative effects on performance for variability models. Finally, the study identifies a research gap: while Algorithm 5 with optimal chunk size can achieve runtime reductions exceeding 50\% compared to Algorithm 2/3 (the one that product line tools implement), the optimal chunk size varies unpredictably across formulas and cannot currently be estimated, opening directions for future research.

</details>


### [92] [Self-Admitted Technical Debt in Scientific Software: Prioritization, Sentiment, and Propagation Across Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15883)
*Eric L. Melin,Nasir U. Eisty,Gregory R. Watson,Addi Malviya-Thakur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析了科学软件中自承认技术债务的优先级、情感、持久性和传播特征，发现注释、提交和拉取请求中的SATD优先级高于问题中的，负面情感增加紧迫性，解决率低于开源软件平均水平，跨制品传播的SATD具有更高优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学软件中的自承认技术债务影响软件质量，但其优先级分配、情感表达、持久性和传播特性尚未得到充分研究。理解科学软件开发者如何表达和处理SATD对于改进软件维护和工具支持至关重要。

Method: 分析9个科学软件仓库，使用SATD分类模型和基于语义嵌入的优先级启发式方法。在多制品中检查SATD，通过微调transformer评估情感，追踪传播路径，比较优先级分数与静态分析，量化移除和解决率。

Result: 注释、提交和拉取请求中的SATD优先级高于问题中的SATD；负面情感增强了紧迫性感知；解决和移除率低于开源软件平均水平；大多数SATD局限于原始制品，但较长的传播链罕见且与更高优先级相关，表明持久且高影响的技术债务。

Conclusion: 优先级受制品类型和情感影响，低移除和解决率表明持久债务。跨制品传播标志着高优先级、未解决的SATD，为科学软件中的针对性监控、审查优先级排序和工具支持维护提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Self-admitted technical debt (SATD) impairs scientific software (SSW), yet its prioritization, sentiment, persistence, and propagation remains underexplored. Understanding how SSW developers express, and address SATD is crucial for improving SSW maintenance, and tooling. This study investigates how SATD types and artifacts in SSW are prioritized, how sentiment relates to urgency, SATD removal and resolution rates, and the extent to which SATD propagates across artifacts. We analyzed nine SSW repositories using a SATD classification model and a semantic embedding-based prioritization heuristic. SATD was examined across multiple artifacts, with sentiment assessed via a fine-tuned transformer. Propagation was traced, priority scores compared to static analysis, and removal and resolution rates quantified. SATD in comments, commits, and pull requests receive higher priority than SATD in issues, with negative sentiment amplifying urgency. Resolution and removal rates lag behind open-source software (OSS) averages. Most SATD remains confined to the originating artifact, but longer propagation chains are rare and correlate with higher priority, highlighting persistent and high impact debt. Prioritization is influenced by artifact type and sentiment, while low removal and resolution rates signal persistent debt. Cross-artifact propagation marks high priority, unresolved SATD, providing empirical guidance for targeted monitoring, review prioritization, and tool supported maintenance in SSW.

</details>


### [93] [VIBEPASS: Can Vibe Coders Really Pass the Vibe Check?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15921)
*Srijan Bansal,Jiao Fangkai,Yilun Zhou,Austin Xu,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统评估LLM在自主软件工程中的故障诊断与修复能力，发现故障针对性推理是主要瓶颈而非代码合成或测试有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型推动编程向"氛围编码"转变，智能编码工具越来越依赖模型自我诊断和修复细微故障，这种自主软件工程的核心能力从未被系统评估过

Method: 提出首个联合评估框架，分解为两个耦合任务：故障触发测试生成（构建暴露潜在错误的判别性见证）和故障针对性程序修复（在不同诊断条件下修复）。使用竞争性编程问题与LLM生成的解决方案配对，这些方案通过部分测试套件但在语义边界情况下失败

Result: 评估12个前沿LLM发现：故障针对性推理能力不随一般编码能力扩展；模型在语法有效测试输入生成上接近上限，但在判别性生成上崩溃；故障假设生成是主要瓶颈而非输出验证；当自生成测试成功见证故障时，修复效果匹配或优于外部测试指导，但未能见证故障的测试会主动降低修复效果

Conclusion: 自主调试的主要挑战被重新定义：核心瓶颈不是代码合成或测试有效性，而是故障针对性推理能力，这一能力在所有前沿模型中仍然不足

Abstract: As Large Language Models shift the programming toward human-guided ''vibe coding'', agentic coding tools increasingly rely on models to self-diagnose and repair their own subtle faults -- a capability central to autonomous software engineering yet never systematically evaluated. We present \name{}, the first empirical decomposition that jointly evaluates two coupled tasks: \emph{Fault-Triggering Test Generation (FT-Test)} constructing a discriminative witness that exposes a latent bug, and \emph{Fault-targeted Program Repair (FPR)}, repairing it under varying diagnostic conditions. \name{} pairs competitive programming problems with LLM-generated solutions that pass partial test suites but fail on semantic edge cases, enabling controlled identification of where the diagnostic chain breaks down.
  Evaluating 12 frontier LLMs, we find that fault-targeted reasoning does not scale with general coding ability. Models produce syntactically valid test inputs at near-ceiling rates yet collapse on discriminative generation, with fault hypothesis generation -- not output validation -- as the dominant bottleneck. Test-guided repair reveals a complementary insight: when self-generated tests successfully witness a fault, the resulting repair matches or outperforms repair guided by externally provided tests, but tests that fail to witness the fault actively degrade repair below unguided baselines. Together, these results reframe the challenge of autonomous debugging: the binding bottleneck is not code synthesis or test validity but fault-target reasoning, a capability that remains deficient across all frontier models. As Large Language Models shift the programming toward human-guided ''vibe coding'', agentic coding tools increasingly rely on models to self-diagnose and repair their own subtle faults -- a capability central to autonomous software engineering yet never systematically evaluated.

</details>


### [94] [Test Code Review in the Era of GitHub Actions: A Replication Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15935)
*Hui Sun,Yinan Wu,Wesley K. G. Assunção,Kathryn T. Stolee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究复制并扩展了Spadini等人的工作，发现在GitHub PR模型中测试代码与生产代码的讨论更平衡，但GitHub Actions的采用导致测试代码审查急剧减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现在Gerrit预提交审查模型中测试代码获得的讨论显著少于生产代码，但最流行的审查模型是基于PR的，且GitHub Actions广泛用于自动化预检查和测试，可能影响审查实践。需要探索Spadini等人的发现在PR模型和GHA时代是否仍然成立。

Method: 复制并扩展Spadini等人的研究，聚焦GitHub PRs，分析六个开源项目，调查PR模型和GHA对测试代码审查的影响。

Result: GitHub的PR模型相比Gerrit促进了测试文件和生产文件之间更平衡的讨论，但总体评论密度较低。然而，尽管存在跨项目异质性，GHA的采用引发了向生产代码的急剧转变。GHA采用后，对于涉及测试的PRs，审查概率和评论密度的中位数都为零。

Conclusion: 不断演进的持续集成流水线可能会边缘化测试代码审查。GHA下测试中心讨论的下降值得关注长期软件质量。研究还提出了软件开发生命周期中相关利益方的建议。

Abstract: Test code is indispensable in software development, ensuring the correctness of production code and supporting maintainability. Nonetheless, errors or omissions in the test code can conceal production defects. While code review is widely adopted to assess code quality and correctness, little research has examined how test code is reviewed. Spadini et al.'s research on Gerrit (a pre-commit review model) found that test code receives significantly less discussion than production code. However, the most popular review model is currently based on pull requests (PRs), in which contributors propose changes for discussion and approval, a more negotiable and flexible model compared to Gerrit. Furthermore, GitHub Actions (GHA) has become widely used to automate pre-checks and testing, potentially impacting review practices. This leads us to explore whether Spadini et al.'s findings still hold for the PR model in the era of GHA? Our work replicates and extends their work. We focus on GitHub PRs and analyze six open-source projects. We investigate the impact of the PR model and GHA on test code review. Our results show that GitHub's PR model fosters more balanced discussions between test and production files than Gerrit, albeit with lower overall comment density. However, despite cross-project heterogeneity, GHA adoption triggered a sharp pivot toward production code. Post-GHA, for PRs involving tests, both review probability and comment density reached a median of zero. These findings reveal how evolving continuous integration pipelines can marginalize test code review. The observed decline in test-centric discussion under GHA warrants concern regarding long-term software quality. Our work also presents recommendations for stakeholders involved in the software development life cycle.

</details>


### [95] [Making Software Metrics Useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16012)
*Ewan Tempero,Paul Ralph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件度量研究缺乏计量学基础，导致实践中难以回答有用问题，需要重新定位研究方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件度量在工程实践中很少被用于实际决策，尽管存在许多度量方法，但无法回答"是否需要重构类"或"集成测试是否充分"等实用问题，导致从业者质疑软件度量的价值

Method: 通过分析当前软件度量研究的局限性，指出其缺乏计量学（测量科学）基础，并提出软件度量研究应采取的改进方向

Result: 识别出软件度量研究的主要问题：开发时未充分考虑计量学原理，导致度量结果难以支持实际工程决策

Conclusion: 软件度量研究需要重新定位，基于计量学原理开发能够真正支持工程决策的度量方法，以提高其在实践中的实用价值

Abstract: Most engineers use measurements to make decisions. However, measurements are rarely used for decisions about constructing software products. While many approaches to measuring attributes of software (``metrics'') have been developed, they are rarely used to answer useful questions such as ``Do I need to refactor this class?'' or ``Are these integration tests sufficient?'' Practitioners therefore question the value of software metrics. We argue that this situation arose because software metrics were developed without understanding metrology (the science of measurement) and suggest directions software metrics research should take.

</details>


### [96] [RepoReviewer: A Local-First Multi-Agent Architecture for Repository-Level Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16107)
*Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoReviewer是一个本地优先的多智能体系统，用于自动化GitHub仓库代码审查，通过分层架构将审查过程分解为多个阶段，避免传统单次审查的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化代码审查工作流通常将仓库级审查任务压缩为单次处理，这会导致相关性降低、重复增加和优先级弱化。需要一种更有效的架构来支持对项目结构、仓库上下文和文件级实现细节的综合推理。

Method: 设计了一个多智能体系统，包含Python CLI、FastAPI API、LangGraph编排层和Next.js用户界面。系统将审查过程分解为五个阶段：仓库获取、上下文合成、文件级分析、发现优先级排序和总结生成。

Result: 提出了RepoReviewer作为一个技术系统贡献，提供了实用的仓库级自动化审查架构，并配备了可重用的评估和报告基础设施，为未来实证研究奠定基础。

Conclusion: RepoReviewer不是追求基准测试优势，而是提供一个实用的系统架构，通过分层多智能体方法解决仓库级代码审查的复杂性，为自动化代码审查领域提供可扩展的技术方案。

Abstract: Repository-level code review requires reasoning over project structure, repository context, and file-level implementation details. Existing automated review workflows often collapse these tasks into a single pass, which can reduce relevance, increase duplication, and weaken prioritization. We present RepoReviewer, a local-first multi-agent system for automated GitHub repository review with a Python CLI, FastAPI API, LangGraph orchestration layer, and Next.js user interface. RepoReviewer decomposes review into repository acquisition, context synthesis, file-level analysis, finding prioritization, and summary generation. We describe the system design, implementation tradeoffs, developer-facing interfaces, and practical failure modes. Rather than claiming benchmark superiority, we frame RepoReviewer as a technical systems contribution: a pragmatic architecture for repository-level automated review, accompanied by reusable evaluation and reporting infrastructure for future empirical study.

</details>


### [97] [SoK: Systematizing Software Artifacts Traceability via Associations, Techniques, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16208)
*Zhifei Chen,Lata Yi,Liming Nie,Yangyang Zhao,Hao Liu,Yiqing Shi,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统文献综述，构建了首个软件制品追踪全局图，揭示了研究不平衡问题，分析了追踪恢复技术向深度语义模型转变的趋势，并量化了工业应用差距，提出了角色中心框架来弥合理论与实践鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件追踪在软件开发中至关重要，但现有研究存在碎片化问题：缺乏对制品关联的完整概览、链接技术模糊、应用场景知识分散。需要系统整合追踪恢复领域的研究成果，为学术界和工业界提供统一的知识框架。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，综合分析了软件追踪恢复领域的相关研究。构建了包含22种制品类型和23种关联的全局追踪图；分析了恢复工具和技术趋势；设计了包含技术决策图和标准化基准的评估框架；提出了角色中心框架来动态对齐制品路径与工程活动。

Result: 1. 揭示了严重的研究不平衡：大部分研究集中在代码相关链接，其他制品关联研究不足；2. 发现技术向深度语义模型转变，但存在可复现性危机（仅37%研究发布代码）；3. 量化了工业应用差距：95%的工具仅限于学术研究；4. 构建了首个全局制品追踪图，提供了技术决策图和标准化基准。

Conclusion: 该综述为软件制品追踪研究提供了连贯的知识框架，识别了当前趋势，并为未来工作指明了方向。通过角色中心框架和标准化评估方法，有助于弥合学术研究与工业实践之间的鸿沟，推动追踪恢复技术的实际应用。

Abstract: Software development relies heavily on traceability links between various software artifacts to ensure quality and facilitate maintenance. While automated traceability recovery techniques have advanced for different artifact pairs, the field remains fragmented with an incomplete overview of artifact associations, ambiguous linking techniques, and fragmented knowledge of application scenarios. To bridge these gaps, we conducted a systematic literature review on software traceability recovery to synthesize the linked artifacts, recovery tools, and usage scenarios across the traceability ecosystem. First, we constructed the first global artifacts traceability graph of 23 associations among 22 artifact types, exposing a severe research imbalance that heavily favors code-related links. Second, while recovery techniques are shifting toward deep semantic models, a reproducibility crisis persists (e.g., only 37% of studies released code); to address this, we provided a comprehensive evaluation framework including a technical decision map and standardized benchmarks. Finally, we quantified an industrial adoption gap (i.e., 95% of tools remain confined to academia) and proposed a role-centric framework to dynamically align artifact paths with concrete engineering activities. This review contributes a coherent knowledge framework for artifacts traceability research, identifies current trends, and provides directions for future work.

</details>


### [98] [GitOps for Capture the Flag Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16265)
*Mikkel Bengtson Albrechtsen,Jacopo Mauro,Torben Worm*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CTF Pilot是一个基于GitOps的CTF竞赛部署管理框架，通过Git仓库作为挑战定义和基础设施配置的单一可信源，实现自动化、版本控制的部署，提升组织者协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CTF竞赛部署管理存在挑战定义与基础设施配置分离、协作困难、部署过程复杂等问题，需要一种能够自动化部署、支持版本控制、促进团队协作的解决方案。

Method: 采用GitOps方法，将Git仓库作为挑战定义和基础设施配置的单一可信源，设计并实现了CTF Pilot框架，支持自动化部署、版本控制和高并发处理能力。

Result: 通过真实CTF赛事评估，CTF Pilot展现出成本效益高、能够有效处理高并发参与者、提供强大的隔离性，并简化了挑战开发流程，改善了组织者和参与者的体验。

Conclusion: CTF Pilot成功证明了GitOps方法在CTF竞赛管理中的有效性，为组织者提供了更高效、可靠的部署管理方案，并指出了未来改进方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we present CTF Pilot, a GitOps-based framework for the deployment and management of Capture The Flag (CTF) competitions. By leveraging Git repositories as the single source of truth for challenge definitions and infrastructure configurations, CTF Pilot enables automated, version-controlled deployments that enhance collaboration among challenge authors and organizers. We detail the design criteria and implementation of CTF Pilot and evaluate our approach through a real-world CTF event, demonstrating its cost efficiency and its effectiveness in handling high participant concurrency while ensuring robust isolation and ease of challenge development. Our results indicate that CTF Pilot improves the experience for organizers and participants, and we present the lessons learned, highlighting opportunities for future improvement.

</details>


### [99] [A Human-Centred Architecture for Large Language Models-Cognitive Assistants in Manufacturing within Quality Management Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16325)
*Marcos Galdino,Johanna Grahl,Tobias Hamann,Anas Abdelrazeq,Ingrid Isenhardt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于组件的人类中心软件架构，用于将大型语言模型认知助手集成到制造质量管理系统中


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献缺乏针对质量管理系统的以人为中心的软件架构，无法有效将LLM认知助手集成到制造环境中，这阻碍了持续过程改进和知识管理

Method: 设计基于组件的软件架构，考虑需求分析和软件开发过程，通过迭代式专家焦点小组进行验证

Result: 提出的架构确保了质量管理系统的灵活性、可扩展性、模块化和工作增强，为工业合作伙伴的实际操作化铺平了道路

Conclusion: 该架构填补了现有研究空白，展示了将LLM认知助手集成到制造质量管理系统的潜力，能够促进制造过程的进步

Abstract: Large Language Models-Cognitive Assistants (LLM-CAs) can enhance Quality Management Systems (QMS) in manufacturing, fostering continuous process improvement and knowledge management. However, there is no human-centred software architecture focused on QMS that enables the integration of LLM-CAs into manufacturing in the current literature. This study addresses this gap by designing a component-based architecture considering requirement analysis and software development process. Validation was conducted via iterative expert focus groups. The proposed architecture ensures flexibility, scalability, modularity, and work augmentation within QMS. Moreover, it paves the way for its operationalization with industrial partners, showcasing its potential for advancing manufacturing processes.

</details>


### [100] [Prompts Blend Requirements and Solutions: From Intent to Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16348)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Jan-Philipp Steghöfer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出将AI编程助手中的提示词重新理解为轻量级、不断演化的需求工件，并提出了包含功能质量、通用解决方案和具体解决方案的三组件概念模型，旨在通过实证研究验证相关假设，最终建立需求感知的提示工程最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI编程助手中的提示实践是非正式和临时的，而需求工程强调捕获、验证和演化需求。作者认为提示词应被视为轻量级、不断演化的需求工件，将需求与解决方案指导相结合，从而将提示工程提升为软件工程的核心关注点。

Method: 提出一个概念模型，将提示词分解为三个相互关联的组件：功能与质量（需求）、通用解决方案（架构策略和技术选择）和具体解决方案（实现级约束）。通过分析现有提示词来评估该模型，并基于模型和初步评估提出四个假设，计划通过真实世界AI辅助开发的数据集分析、语料库分析和受控实验进行实证验证。

Result: 提出了一个概念模型，展示了提示词中三个组件如何在实践中体现。基于模型和初步评估，提出了四个假设：提示词向具体性演化、演化因用户特征而异、使用提示的工程师增加了需求验证和确认、渐进式提示细化产生更高质量的代码。

Conclusion: 通过需求工程的视角重新思考提示词，将其定位为软件工程未来的核心关注点，而不仅仅是技术技能。计划通过实证研究验证假设，最终推导出需求感知的提示工程最佳实践，从而将提示工程提升为软件工程学科的重要组成部分。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are reshaping software development by shifting focus from writing code to formulating prompts. In chat-focused approaches such as vibe coding, prompts become the primary arbiter between human intent and executable software. While Requirements Engineering (RE) emphasizes capturing, validating, and evolving requirements, current prompting practices remain informal and adhoc. We argue that prompts should be understood as lightweight, evolving requirement artifacts that blend requirements with solution guidance. We propose a conceptual model decomposing prompts into three interrelated components: Functionality and Quality (the requirement), General Solutions (architectural strategy and technology choices) and Specific Solutions (implementation-level constraints). We assess this model using existing prompts, examining how these components manifest in practice. Based on this model and the initial assessment, we formulate four hypotheses: prompts evolve toward specificity, evolution varies by user characteristics, engineers using prompting engage in increased requirement validation and verification, and progressive prompt refinement yields higher code quality. Our vision is to empirically evaluate these hypotheses through analysis of real-world AI-assisted development, with datasets, corpus analysis, and controlled experiments, ultimately deriving best practices for requirements-aware prompt engineering. By rethinking prompts through the lens of RE, we position prompting not merely as a technical skill, but as a central concern for software engineering's future.

</details>


### [101] [InCoder-32B: Code Foundation Model for Industrial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16790)
*Jian Yang,Wei Zhang,Jiajun Wu,Junhang Cheng,Shawn Guo,Haowen Wang,Weicheng Gu,Yaxin Du,Joseph Li,Fanglin Xu,Yizhi Li,Lin Jing,Yuanbo Wang,Yuhan Gao,Ruihao Gong,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Aishan Liu,Tuney Zheng,Ganqu Cui,Zhoujun Li,Mingjie Tang,Chenghua Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Xianglong Liu,Ming Zhou,Bryan Dai,Weifeng Lv*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: InCoder-32B是首个32B参数的工业代码基础模型，在芯片设计、GPU内核优化、嵌入式系统、编译器优化和3D建模等工业场景中统一代码智能，通过多阶段训练策略在保持通用编程能力的同时显著提升工业代码理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型代码语言模型在通用编程任务上表现出色，但在需要理解硬件语义、专业语言构造和严格资源约束的工业场景中性能显著下降。工业场景的特殊需求（如芯片设计、嵌入式系统等）要求模型具备领域专业知识，而现有模型在这方面存在不足。

Method: 采用高效架构，从零开始训练InCoder-32B，包含四个阶段：1) 通用代码预训练；2) 精选工业代码退火训练；3) 中期训练，使用合成工业推理数据将上下文从8K逐步扩展到128K令牌；4) 基于执行验证的后训练。

Result: 在14个主流通用代码基准和9个工业基准（涵盖4个专业领域）上进行评估。结果显示InCoder-32B在通用任务上具有高度竞争力，同时在工业领域建立了强大的开源基线。

Conclusion: InCoder-32B成功解决了工业代码场景的挑战，通过统一的多领域训练方法，在保持通用编程能力的同时显著提升了工业代码理解和生成能力，为工业代码智能应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent code large language models have achieved remarkable progress on general programming tasks. Nevertheless, their performance degrades significantly in industrial scenarios that require reasoning about hardware semantics, specialized language constructs, and strict resource constraints. To address these challenges, we introduce InCoder-32B (Industrial-Coder-32B), the first 32B-parameter code foundation model unifying code intelligence across chip design, GPU kernel optimization, embedded systems, compiler optimization, and 3D modeling. By adopting an efficient architecture, we train InCoder-32B from scratch with general code pre-training, curated industrial code annealing, mid-training that progressively extends context from 8K to 128K tokens with synthetic industrial reasoning data, and post-training with execution-grounded verification. We conduct extensive evaluation on 14 mainstream general code benchmarks and 9 industrial benchmarks spanning 4 specialized domains. Results show InCoder-32B achieves highly competitive performance on general tasks while establishing strong open-source baselines across industrial domains.

</details>


### [102] [Improving Code Comprehension through Cognitive-Load Aware Automated Refactoring for Novice Programmers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16791)
*Subarna Saha,Alif Al Hasan,Fariha Tanjim Shifat,Mia Mohammad Imran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CDDRefactorER：基于认知驱动开发的自动代码重构方法，通过降低控制流复杂度来提升新手程序员的代码理解能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新手程序员常因代码命名模糊、嵌套过深和结构组织不良而难以理解代码。现有解释方法通常不重构代码本身，因此需要一种能自动重构代码以提升清晰度的认知脚手架方法。

Method: 提出CDDRefactorER方法，基于认知驱动开发（Cognitive-Driven Development），通过约束代码转换来降低控制流复杂度，同时保持行为不变和结构相似性。该方法在MBPP和APPS基准数据集上评估，使用gpt-5-nano和kimi-k2模型，并进行受控人类主体研究。

Result: CDDRefactorER将重构失败率降低了54-71%，显著降低了重构过程中圈复杂度和认知复杂度增加的可能性。人类研究显示，新手代码理解能力得到一致提升：函数识别能力提高31.3%，结构可读性提高22.0%。

Conclusion: 认知引导的重构为增强新手代码理解提供了实用有效的机制，CDDRefactorER方法在自动代码重构方面表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Novice programmers often struggle to comprehend code due to vague naming, deep nesting, and poor structural organization. While explanations may offer partial support, they typically do not restructure the code itself. We propose code refactoring as cognitive scaffolding, where cognitively guided refactoring automatically restructures code to improve clarity. We operationalize this in CDDRefactorER, an automated approach grounded in Cognitive-Driven Development that constrains transformations to reduce control-flow complexity while preserving behavior and structural similarity.
  We evaluate CDDRefactorER using two benchmark datasets (MBPP and APPS) against two models (gpt-5-nano and kimi-k2), and a controlled human-subject study with novice programmers. Across datasets and models, CDDRefactorER reduces refactoring failures by 54-71% and substantially lowers the likelihood of increased Cyclomatic and Cognitive complexity during refactoring, compared to unconstrained prompting. Results from the human study show consistent improvements in novice code comprehension, with function identification increasing by 31.3% and structural readability by 22.0%. The findings suggest that cognitively guided refactoring offers a practical and effective mechanism for enhancing novice code comprehension.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Constructing Weakly Terminating Interface Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15675)
*Debjyoti Bera,Tim A. C. Willemse*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了现有保证弱终止接口组合的理论，使其更广泛适用于实践，通过部分镜像关系推导客户端模型，并提供了开源工具支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步通信系统中的高并发性增加了死锁和活锁的风险，弱终止属性作为基本健全性检查可以避免这些问题。现有保证弱终止接口组合的理论适用范围有限，需要扩展以更广泛地应用于实践。

Method: 从满足特定属性的服务器接口规范出发，使用部分镜像关系推导出模拟不同使用场景的客户端类。将理论结果嵌入开源工具中，指导建模者设计弱终止接口。

Result: 扩展了现有保证弱终止接口组合的理论，使其具有更广泛的适用性。开发了基于部分镜像关系的客户端推导方法，并实现了开源工具支持。

Conclusion: 该研究通过理论扩展和工具实现，使弱终止接口设计方法更实用，有助于在异步通信系统中避免死锁和活锁问题，提高系统可靠性。

Abstract: Interfaces play a central role in determining compatible component compositions by prescribing permissible interactions between a service provider (server) and its consumers (clients). The high degree of concurrency in asynchronous communicating systems increases the risk of unintentionally introducing deadlocks and livelocks. The weak termination property serves as a basic sanity check to avoid such problems. It assures that in each reachable state, the system has the option to eventually terminate. This paper generalizes existing results that, by construction, guarantee weakly terminating interface compositions. Our generalizations make the theory applicable more broadly in practice. Starting with an interface specification of a server satisfying certain properties, we show how a class of clients modeling different usage contexts can be derived using a partial mirroring relation. Furthermore, we discuss an embedding of our results in an open-source tool to guide modelers in designing weakly terminating interfaces.

</details>


### [104] [A Non-Binary Method for Finding Interpolants: Theory and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15876)
*Adam Trybus,Karolina Rożko,Tomasz Skura*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于反驳系统寻找经典逻辑插值的新方法，该方法可视为非二元消解法的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳系统作为形式系统分析的替代方法，关注哪些公式应被拒绝而非证明，这种镜像证明系统的方法具有超出反驳演算本身的应用价值。

Method: 以特定反驳系统为起点，开发插值搜索方法，该方法可视为基于非二元消解法的变体。

Result: 提出了一种新的插值寻找方法，该方法利用反驳系统的特性，为经典逻辑提供了一种基于非二元消解法的插值搜索技术。

Conclusion: 反驳系统方法不仅限于反驳演算本身，还能为插值问题提供新颖的解决方案，展示了该方法在逻辑分析中的广泛适用性。

Abstract: We describe a new method of finding interpolants for classical logic using certain refutation system as a starting point. Refutation can be thought of as an alternative approach to the analysis of formal systems: instead of focusing on which formulas provably belong to a given logic, it shows which formulas are to be rejected. Thus, it provides a mirror proof system. As it turns out, the benefits of such an approach go well beyond the area of refutation calculi themselves. We provide one such example in the shape of an interpolant-searching method. To be sure, a number of such methods are already in use. The novelty of our proposal lies in the fact that it can be considered as based on a non-binary version of resolution.

</details>


### [105] [Three-Dimensional Affine Spatial Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16308)
*Adam Trybus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于区域的仿射几何空间逻辑，证明不同维度的逻辑具有不同理论，并重点分析三维情况，展示其表达能力足以描述三维坐标系，最终证明每个区域都满足仿射完全公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于区域的仿射几何空间逻辑，该领域研究稀缺，主要局限于实平面。长期目标是分析包含包含关系和凸性作为原始概念的仿射逻辑族，在不同维度的实空间中进行解释。

Method: 首先证明不同维度的逻辑必须具有不同的理论，为不同维度的工作提供理论依据。然后聚焦三维情况，探索该逻辑的表达能力，展示如何构造描述三维坐标系的公式。最后利用该逻辑的高表达能力，证明每个区域都满足仿射完全公式。

Result: 1. 证明了不同维度的仿射逻辑具有不同的理论；2. 在三维情况下展示了该逻辑的强大表达能力，能够构造描述三维坐标系的公式；3. 证明了每个区域都满足仿射完全公式，意味着所有满足该公式的区域都是仿射等价的。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于区域的仿射几何空间逻辑提供了重要的理论进展，证明了维度差异对逻辑理论的影响，展示了三维情况下的表达能力，并建立了仿射完全性的重要结果，为进一步研究不同维度的仿射逻辑奠定了基础。

Abstract: We focus on a branch of region-based spatial logics dealing with affine geometry. The research on this topic is scarce: only a handful of papers investigate such systems, mostly in the case of the real plane. Our long-term goal is to analyse certain family of affine logics with inclusion and convexity as primitives interpreted over real spaces of increasing dimensionality. In this article we show that logics of different dimensionalities must have different theories, thus justifying further work on different dimensions. We then focus on the three-dimensional case, exploring the expressiveness of this logic and consequently showing that it is possible to construct formulas describing a three-dimensional coordinate frame. The final result, making use of the high expressive power of this logic, is that every region satisfies an affine complete formula, meaning that all regions satisfying it are affine equivalent.

</details>


### [106] [Monoidal categories graded by partial commutative monoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16375)
*Matthew Earnshaw,Chad Nester,Mario Román*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于偏交换幺半群（PCM）分级的幺半范畴理论，将效应范畴和幺半范畴统一为特例，并应用于并行计算和资源受限建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 效应范畴包含纯态射和效应态射两类，其中效应态射只能与中心态射（如纯态射）进行幺半组合，形成预幺半范畴。这启发我们将态射视为带有"等级"的结构，这些等级在幺半积下以部分定义的方式组合。论文旨在形式化这一思想，建立统一的数学框架。

Method: 引入偏交换幺半群（PCM）分级幺半范畴的概念，将幺半范畴视为单元素PCM的特例，效应范畴视为两元素PCM的特例。通过幂集PCM建模共享资源访问的非干扰并行性，通过区间PCM建模有界资源使用。证明效应范畴构成PCM分级幺半范畴的核心反射子范畴，引入笛卡尔结构恢复Freyd范畴，并将PCM分级幺半范畴描述为将PCM视为薄预幺半范畴的幺半对象。

Result: 建立了统一的PCM分级幺半范畴理论框架，将效应范畴和幺半范畴统一为特例。展示了幂集PCM和区间PCM的具体应用实例。证明了效应范畴是PCM分级幺半范畴的核心反射子范畴，并建立了与Freyd范畴的联系。

Conclusion: PCM分级幺半范畴为效应范畴和幺半范畴提供了统一的数学框架，能够建模各种计算效应和资源约束。该框架具有广泛的应用前景，包括并行计算、资源受限系统和效应系统理论。

Abstract: Effectful categories have two classes of morphisms: pure morphisms, which form a monoidal category; and effectful morphisms, which can only be combined monoidally with central morphisms (such as the pure ones), forming a premonoidal category. This suggests seeing morphisms of an effectful category as carrying a grade that combines under the monoidal product in a partially defined manner. We axiomatize this idea with the notion of monoidal category graded by a partial commutative monoid (PCM). Monoidal categories arise as the special case of grading by the singleton PCM, and effectful categories arise from grading by a two-element PCM. Further examples include grading by powerset PCMs, modelling non-interfering parallelism for programs accessing shared resources, and grading by intervals, modelling bounded resource usage. We show that effectful categories form a coreflective subcategory of PCM-graded monoidal categories; introduce cartesian structure, recovering Freyd categories; and describe PCM-graded monoidal categories as monoids by viewing a PCM as a thin promonoidal category.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Efficient LLM Serving for Agentic Workflows: A Data Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16104)
*Noppanat Wadlom,Junyi Shen,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Helium是一个面向智能体工作流的LLM服务框架，通过将工作流建模为查询计划、集成主动缓存和缓存感知调度，实现了跨提示、KV状态和工作流的重用优化，相比现有系统获得最高1.56倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统（如vLLM）主要优化单个推理调用，忽视了智能体工作流中跨调用依赖关系导致的冗余，包括重叠提示和中间结果，这造成了显著的效率低下。

Method: Helium从数据系统视角重新思考LLM和智能体服务，将智能体工作流建模为查询计划，将LLM调用视为一等操作符，集成主动缓存和缓存感知调度来最大化跨提示、KV状态和工作流的重用。

Result: Helium在各种工作负载上相比最先进的智能体服务系统实现了最高1.56倍的加速，证明了跨工作流的端到端优化对于可扩展和高效的基于LLM的智能体至关重要。

Conclusion: 将经典查询优化原则与LLM服务相结合，通过工作流感知的服务框架可以实现显著的性能提升，跨工作流的端到端优化是构建可扩展高效LLM智能体系统的关键。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are composed of sequences of interdependent Large Language Model (LLM) calls, and they have become a dominant workload in modern AI systems. These workflows exhibit extensive redundancy from overlapping prompts and intermediate results due to speculative and parallel exploration. Existing LLM serving systems, such as vLLM, focus on optimizing individual inference calls and overlook cross-call dependencies, leading to significant inefficiencies. This paper rethinks LLM and agent serving from a data systems perspective and introduces Helium, a workflow-aware serving framework that models agentic workloads as query plans and treats LLM invocations as first-class operators. Helium integrates proactive caching and cache-aware scheduling to maximize reuse across prompts, KV states, and workflows. Through these techniques, Helium bridges classic query optimization principles with LLM serving, achieving up to 1.56x speedup over state-of-the-art agent serving systems on various workloads. Our results demonstrate that end-to-end optimization across workflows is essential for scalable and efficient LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [108] [Communication-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Decentralized Cooperative UAV Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16141)
*Enguang Fan,Yifan Chen,Zihan Shan,Matthew Caesar,Jae Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出基于图的多智能体强化学习框架，用于部分可观测和间歇通信条件下的无人机群协同中继和对抗任务，采用集中训练分散执行架构，通过注意力机制编码局部状态和邻居信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机群作为快速部署的中继和感知平台日益重要，但实际部署面临部分可观测性和间歇性点对点通信的挑战，需要能在这些限制下有效协同的算法框架。

Method: 采用集中训练分散执行的多智能体强化学习框架：训练时使用集中式评论器和全局状态，执行时每个无人机使用共享策略处理局部观测和邻居消息。架构包含智能体-实体注意力模块编码局部状态和附近实体，以及基于距离限制通信图的邻居自注意力模块聚合无人机间消息。

Result: 在DroneConnect中继任务中，方法在受限通信和部分观测下实现高覆盖率（如5架无人机、10个节点时74%覆盖率），与混合整数线性规划离线上界竞争，且无需微调即可泛化到未见过的团队规模。在DroneCombat对抗任务中，同一框架无需架构修改即可迁移，相比无通信基线提高胜率。

Conclusion: 提出的图基多智能体强化学习框架能有效处理部分可观测和间歇通信的无人机群协同任务，在中继和对抗场景中均表现良好，具有泛化能力和可迁移性。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms are increasingly used as rapidly deployable aerial relays and sensing platforms, yet practical deployments must operate under partial observability and intermittent peer-to-peer links. We present a graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning framework trained under centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE): a centralized critic and global state are available only during training, while each UAV executes a shared policy using local observations and messages from nearby neighbors. Our architecture encodes local agent state and nearby entities with an agent-entity attention module, and aggregates inter-UAV messages with neighbor self-attention over a distance-limited communication graph. We evaluate primarily on a cooperative relay deployment task (DroneConnect) and secondarily on an adversarial engagement task (DroneCombat). In DroneConnect, the proposed method achieves high coverage under restricted communication and partial observation (e.g. 74% coverage with M = 5 UAVs and N = 10 nodes) while remaining competitive with a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimization-based offline upper bound, and it generalizes to unseen team sizes without fine-tuning. In the adversarial setting, the same framework transfers without architectural changes and improves win rate over non-communicating baselines.

</details>


### [109] [CoMAI: A Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Robust and Equitable Interview Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16215)
*Gengxin Sun,Ruihao Yu,Liangyi Yin,Yunqi Yang,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CoMAI是一个基于多智能体架构的AI面试评估框架，通过任务分解和集中式有限状态机协调，提供多层安全防御和多维评估，相比单智能体系统具有更好的鲁棒性、公平性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的单智能体面试评估系统存在鲁棒性不足、主观偏见、安全漏洞等问题，需要一种更可靠、公平且可解释的AI驱动面试评估范式。

Method: 采用模块化任务分解架构，通过集中式有限状态机协调四个专门化智能体：问题生成、安全、评分和总结智能体，实现多层安全防御、自适应难度调整和基于评分标准的结构化评分。

Result: 实验结果显示CoMAI达到90.47%准确率、83.33%召回率和84.41%候选人满意度，显著优于传统单智能体系统。

Conclusion: CoMAI为AI驱动的面试评估提供了一个鲁棒、公平且可解释的新范式，通过多智能体协作有效解决了传统系统的局限性。

Abstract: Ensuring robust and fair interview assessment remains a key challenge in AI-driven evaluation. This paper presents CoMAI, a general-purpose multi-agent interview framework designed for diverse assessment scenarios. In contrast to monolithic single-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs), CoMAI employs a modular task-decomposition architecture coordinated through a centralized finite-state machine. The system comprises four agents specialized in question generation, security, scoring, and summarization. These agents work collaboratively to provide multi-layered security defenses against prompt injection, support multidimensional evaluation with adaptive difficulty adjustment, and enable rubric-based structured scoring that reduces subjective bias. Experimental results demonstrate that CoMAI achieved 90.47% accuracy, 83.33% recall, and 84.41% candidate satisfaction. These results highlight CoMAI as a robust, fair, and interpretable paradigm for AI-driven interview assessment.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Computing the connected components of real algebraic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16283)
*Elisabetta Rocchi,Mohab Safey El Din*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 提出一种计算实代数曲线半代数描述的新算法，其复杂度低于已知的用于计算与实空间曲线同胚的图的方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实代数集的连通分量是半代数的，即它们可以通过布尔公式描述，其中原子是具有实系数的多项式约束。计算这种描述在光学系统设计和机器人学中有实际应用。

Method: 设计了一种新的算法来计算实代数曲线的半代数描述。该算法的复杂度低于已知的用于计算与实空间曲线同胚的图的方法。

Result: 算法实现了对实代数曲线半代数描述的计算，其计算复杂度优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 提出的新算法在计算实代数曲线的半代数描述方面具有更低的复杂度，为光学系统设计和机器人学等领域的应用提供了更高效的计算工具。

Abstract: Connected components of real algebraic sets are semi-algebraic, i.e. they are described by a boolean formula whose atoms are polynomial constraints with real coefficients. Computing such descriptions finds topical applications in optical system design and robotics. In this paper, we design a new algorithm for computing such semi-algebraic descriptions for real algebraic curves. Notably, its complexity is less than the best known one for computing a graph which is isotopic to the real space curve under study.

</details>


### [111] [A complexity analysis of the F4 Gröbner basis algorithm with tracer data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16378)
*Robin Kouba,Vincent Neiger,Mohab Safey El Din*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 该论文改进了零维理想在grevlex序下的Gröbner基计算复杂度，基于Moreno-Socías猜想，通过分析正则序列的递增度性质和F4算法中项对的选择，获得了比现有最优界指数级改进的复杂度公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零维理想Gröbner基计算复杂度界限不够精确，特别是在grevlex序下。作者旨在利用Moreno-Socías猜想获得更精确的复杂度分析，显著改进现有结果。

Method: 1. 形式化正则序列的递增度性质；2. 基于Moreno-Socías猜想分析F4算法中项对选择策略；3. 推导grevlex Gröbner基中特定度数的元素数量精确公式；4. 结合这些结果推导F4 Tracer算法的精确复杂度公式及其渐近行为。

Result: 获得了F4 Tracer算法的精确复杂度公式，当变量数趋于无穷时的渐近行为，以及比现有最优复杂度界限指数级改进的结果（改进因子为变量数的指数函数）。

Conclusion: 该研究基于Moreno-Socías猜想显著改进了零维理想grevlex Gröbner基计算的复杂度分析，为符号计算领域提供了重要的理论进展。

Abstract: We provide a new complexity bound for the computation of grevlex Gröbner bases in the generic zero-dimensional case, relying on Moreno-Socías' conjecture. We first formalize a property of regular sequences that implies a well-known folklore consequence, which we call the increasing degree property. We then derive a new understanding of the selection of pairs in the F4 algorithm based on Moreno-Socías' conjecture. Moreover, we obtain an exact formula for the number of elements in the grevlex Gröbner basis of a given degree, for half of the relevant degrees. Combining these results, we derive a precise complexity formula for the F4 Tracer algorithm, together with its asymptotic behavior when the number of variables tends to infinity. These results yield an improvement over the state-of-the-art complexity bounds by a factor which is exponential in the number of variables.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Early-Terminable Energy-Safe Iterative Coupling for Parallel Simulation of Port-Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16424)
*Qi Wei,Jianfeng Tao,Hongyu Nie,Wangtao Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Douglas-Rachford分裂的波域能量安全迭代耦合方法，用于大规模机器人系统的并行仿真与控制，确保在有限迭代次数下保持离散无源性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行仿真中，有限迭代耦合会引入虚假能量，破坏功率一致性，即使每个子系统都是无源的。现有方法无法在有限计算预算下保证能量安全性。

Method: 采用波（散射）坐标下的Douglas-Rachford分裂方案，将无损互连约束表示为波域正交约束，每个子系统提供由其一步积分器诱导的离散时间散射端口映射。

Result: 在子系统时间步满足离散无源性条件和温和阻抗调谐条件下，证明了耦合宏步的离散无源性，剩余失配由显式残差捕获。随着内迭代次数增加，分区更新收敛于相同积分器诱导的整体离散时间更新。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了原则性的自适应精度-计算权衡，支持变化计算预算下的能量一致实时并行仿真，耦合振荡器基准实验验证了无源性证书和误差单调衰减特性。

Abstract: Parallel simulation and control of large-scale robotic systems often rely on partitioned time stepping, yet finite-iteration coupling can inject spurious energy by violating power consistency--even when each subsystem is passive. This letter proposes a novel energy-safe, early-terminable iterative coupling for port-Hamiltonian subsystems by embedding a Douglas--Rachford (DR) splitting scheme in scattering (wave) coordinates. The lossless interconnection is enforced as an orthogonal constraint in the wave domain, while each subsystem contributes a discrete-time scattering port map induced by its one-step integrator. Under a discrete passivity condition on the subsystem time steps and a mild impedance-tuning condition, we prove an augmented-storage inequality certifying discrete passivity of the coupled macro-step for any finite inner-iteration budget, with the remaining mismatch captured by an explicit residual. As the inner budget increases, the partitioned update converges to the monolithic discrete-time update induced by the same integrators, yielding a principled, adaptive accuracy--compute trade-off, supporting energy-consistent real-time parallel simulation under varying computational budgets. Experiments on a coupled-oscillator benchmark validate the passivity certificates at numerical roundoff (on the order of 10e-14 in double precision) and show that the reported RMS state error decays monotonically with increasing inner-iteration budgets, consistent with the hard-coupling limit.

</details>


### [113] [Simulation Distillation: Pretraining World Models in Simulation for Rapid Real-World Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15759)
*Jacob Levy,Tyler Westenbroek,Kevin Huang,Fernando Palafox,Patrick Yin,Shayegan Omidshafiei,Dong-Ki Kim,Abhishek Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimDist是一种从仿真到现实的迁移框架，通过将仿真器中的结构先验蒸馏到潜在世界模型中，实现在线规划和监督式动力学微调，从而在低数据条件下实现快速现实世界适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到现实的迁移是机器人学中的核心挑战，仿真与现实世界动力学之间的不匹配常导致失败。现有方法在低数据条件下难以处理探索和长时程信用分配问题。

Method: SimDist框架将仿真器中的结构先验蒸馏到潜在世界模型中，通过在线规划和监督式动力学微调实现快速适应。直接从仿真迁移奖励和价值模型，无需在部署过程中学习价值函数。

Result: 在精确操作和四足机器人运动任务中，SimDist在数据效率、稳定性和最终性能方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SimDist通过将现实世界适应简化为短时程系统辨识，避免了长时程信用分配问题，实现了快速稳定的性能提升，为仿真到现实迁移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Simulation-to-real transfer remains a central challenge in robotics, as mismatches between simulated and real-world dynamics often lead to failures. While reinforcement learning offers a principled mechanism for adaptation, existing sim-to-real finetuning methods struggle with exploration and long-horizon credit assignment in the low-data regimes typical of real-world robotics. We introduce Simulation Distillation (SimDist), a sim-to-real framework that distills structural priors from a simulator into a latent world model and enables rapid real-world adaptation via online planning and supervised dynamics finetuning. By transferring reward and value models directly from simulation, SimDist provides dense planning signals from raw perception without requiring value learning during deployment. As a result, real-world adaptation reduces to short-horizon system identification, avoiding long-horizon credit assignment and enabling fast, stable improvement. Across precise manipulation and quadruped locomotion tasks, SimDist substantially outperforms prior methods in data efficiency, stability, and final performance. Project website and code: https://sim-dist.github.io/

</details>


### [114] [CorrectionPlanner: Self-Correction Planner with Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15771)
*Yihong Guo,Dongqiangzi Ye,Sijia Chen,Anqi Liu,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CorrectionPlanner：一种具有自校正能力的自动驾驶规划器，通过"提议-评估-校正"循环将规划建模为运动令牌生成，显著降低碰撞率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的自动驾驶规划器缺乏显式的自校正能力，一旦提出不安全动作，没有机制进行校正，这限制了规划的安全性

Method: 提出CorrectionPlanner，采用自回归规划器架构，将规划建模为运动令牌生成。在"提议-评估-校正"循环中：1）策略提议动作（运动令牌）；2）学习的碰撞批评器预测是否会在短时域内引发碰撞；3）若预测碰撞，保留历史不安全运动令牌作为自校正轨迹，基于此生成下一个运动令牌，重复直到安全或满足安全标准。使用模仿学习训练，然后通过预训练世界模型的rollouts进行基于模型的强化学习

Result: 在Waymax上碰撞率降低超过20%，在nuPlan上达到最先进的规划分数。自校正轨迹（不安全运动令牌序列）在运动令牌空间中表示规划器的校正过程，类似于语言模型中的推理轨迹

Conclusion: CorrectionPlanner通过引入显式自校正机制，显著提升了自动驾驶规划的安全性，在多个基准测试中表现出优越性能，为学习型规划器提供了有效的安全增强框架

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires safe planning, but most learning-based planners lack explicit self-correction ability: once an unsafe action is proposed, there is no mechanism to correct it. Thus, we propose CorrectionPlanner, an autoregressive planner with self-correction that models planning as motion-token generation within a propose, evaluate, and correct loop. At each planning step, the policy proposes an action, namely a motion token, and a learned collision critic predicts whether it will induce a collision within a short horizon. If the critic predicts a collision, we retain the sequence of historical unsafe motion tokens as a self-correction trace, generate the next motion token conditioned on it, and repeat this process until a safe motion token is proposed or the safety criterion is met. This self-correction trace, consisting of all unsafe motion tokens, represents the planner's correction process in motion-token space, analogous to a reasoning trace in language models. We train the planner with imitation learning followed by model-based reinforcement learning using rollouts from a pretrained world model that realistically models agents' reactive behaviors. Closed-loop evaluations show that CorrectionPlanner reduces collision rate by over 20% on Waymax and achieves state-of-the-art planning scores on nuPlan.

</details>


### [115] [Emergent Dexterity via Diverse Resets and Large-Scale Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15789)
*Patrick Yin,Tyler Westenbroek,Zhengyu Zhang,Joshua Tran,Ignacio Dagnino,Eeshani Shilamkar,Numfor Mbiziwo-Tiapo,Simran Bagaria,Xinlei Liu,Galen Mullins,Andrey Kolobov,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OmniReset框架，通过程序化生成模拟器重置来系统性地暴露RL算法于多样化的机器人-物体交互，从而简化长视野探索，实现无需人工设计奖励、课程或演示的灵巧操作任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大规模并行物理模拟的强化学习方法存在脆弱性和任务特定性，需要大量人工工程来设计奖励、课程和演示。即使如此，这些方法在处理长视野、接触丰富的操作任务时仍然失败，且计算资源利用率低，性能在重复访问相同状态空间区域后快速饱和。

Method: 提出OmniReset框架，核心洞察是通过模拟器重置系统性地将RL算法暴露于构成灵巧操作基础的多样化机器人-物体交互。该方法以最少人工输入程序化生成重置，将额外计算直接转化为更广泛的行为覆盖和持续性能提升。使用单一奖励函数、固定算法超参数、无课程和无人工演示。

Result: OmniReset能够优雅地扩展到超越现有方法能力的长视野灵巧操作任务，学习到的策略在比基线更广泛的初始条件范围内具有鲁棒性。将策略提炼为视觉运动策略后，在零样本转移到真实世界时表现出鲁棒的重试行为和显著更高的成功率。

Conclusion: OmniReset提供了一个简单且可扩展的框架，使在线策略强化学习能够鲁棒地解决广泛类别的灵巧操作任务，无需人工工程干预，并能将额外计算直接转化为性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in massively parallel physics simulations has driven major progress in sim-to-real robot learning. However, current approaches remain brittle and task-specific, relying on extensive per-task engineering to design rewards, curricula, and demonstrations. Even with this engineering, they often fail on long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation tasks and do not meaningfully scale with compute, as performance quickly saturates when training revisits the same narrow regions of state space.
